\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}=\sum^{np-l}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}t_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}})\,e^{at_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}+(-1)^{j}\sum^{np-l}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,t_{\mathchoice{k+2l}{k+2l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k+2l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k+2l$}}})\,e^{at_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}}:=\widetilde{\upsilon}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}+(-1)^{j}\widehat{\upsilon}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\,,

where
\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=2\pi[j/2]
. So, setting

\widetilde{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\frac{1}{np^{2}}\,\sum^{np-1}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,\widetilde{\upsilon}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\frac{1}{np^{2}}\,\sum^{np-1}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,\widehat{\upsilon}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\,,

we can represent
A_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}
in the form

A_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\widetilde{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}+(-1)^{j}\widehat{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}
, in which

\widetilde{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\,\frac{1}{p}{\cal R}e\frac{\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\,-\widetilde{B}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\frac{1-e^{an}}{np^{2}}\,\widehat{B}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,.

(7.6)

Here

\widetilde{B}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\,\frac{1-e^{an}}{np^{2}}\,{\cal R}e\frac{\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{(1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{2}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widehat{B}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\,{\cal R}e\,\frac{\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\widehat{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{(1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})(1-\widehat{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})}\,,

where

\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=e^{a/p+i\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p}
and

\widehat{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=e^{-a/p+i\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p}
.
First, note that

{\cal R}e\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}=\frac{1-e^{a/p}\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)}{1-2e^{a/p}\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)+e^{2a/p}}=\frac{1-e^{a/p}+2e^{a/p}\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))}{(1-e^{a/p})^{2}+4e^{a/p}\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}a/(2p))}\,.

Taking into account that

\sin(x)\geq 2x/\pi,\quad 0\leq x\leq\pi/2\,,

and

\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\geq 2\pi j/3\,,\quad 2\leq j\leq p\,,

we obtain for
2\leq j\leq p

2\,\sin(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))\geq j/p\,.

(7.7)

Therefore,

\frac{1}{p}\,\left|{\cal R}e\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\right|\,\leq\frac{|a|/p}{4pe^{a/p}\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))}+\frac{1}{2p}\,\leq\frac{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}}{j^{2}}\,+\,\frac{1}{2p}\,.

(7.8)

Now we estimate
\widetilde{B}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}
. We have

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-e^{an})e^{a/p}\left(\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)-2e^{a/p}+e^{2a/p}\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)\right)}{np^{2}\left(1-2e^{a/p}\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)+e^{2a/p}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-e^{an})e^{a/p}\left(\left(1-e^{a/p}\right)^{2}-2\left(1+e^{2a/p}\right)\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))\right)}{np^{2}\left(\left(1-e^{a/p}\right)^{2}+4e^{a/p}\,\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))\right)^{2}}\,.

Applying the inequality (
7.7
) yields

\displaystyle|\widetilde{B}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|a|^{2}}{16p^{4}e^{a/p}\sin^{4}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))}+\frac{1}{4p^{2}e^{a/p}\sin^{2}(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/(2p))}

\displaystyle\leq\,e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\left(a^{2}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\,j^{-4}+j^{-2}\right)\,.

(7.9)

By making use of the estimates (
7.8
) and (
7.9
)
in (
7.6
) we obtain

\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,|\widetilde{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|\leq\frac{1}{2p}+\frac{(a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}+1)e^{a^{max}}}{j^{2}}+\frac{a^{2}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}e^{a^{max}}}{j^{4}}\,.

(7.10)

To estimate
\widehat{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}
we represent
\widehat{B}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
as

\widehat{B}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\frac{\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)}{2(\cos(\alpha_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/p)-\mbox{ch}(a/p))}\,,

where
\mbox{ch}(x)=(e^{x}+e^{-x})/2
. From here and
(
7.7
), it follows that

|\widehat{A}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|\leq\,\leq\frac{1}{2np^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\cos(\varpi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\Delta)}\right)=\frac{1}{4np^{2}\sin^{2}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\Delta/2)}\leq\,\frac{1}{nj^{2}}\,.

(7.11)

Combining (
7.10
) and (
7.11
) yields

p^{-1}\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,|A_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|\leq{\bf A}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\left(p^{-1}+j^{-2}\right)\,,\quad{\bf A}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=1+(1+a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}+a^{2}_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}})e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,.

This and (
7.4
), in view of (
7.5
), implies that

j^{-2}\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,|\tau_{\mathchoice{1,j}{1,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,j$}}}|\leq\,\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\,\left(p^{-1}+j^{-2}\right)\,,\quad\tau^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\left(1+e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,{\bf A}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right)\,.

(7.12)

It remains to estimate
\tau_{\mathchoice{2,j}{2,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,j$}}}
in (
7.3
). First we note
that

\tau_{\mathchoice{2,j}{2,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,j$}}}=\frac{a}{2n}\,\iota^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\iota_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)\,e^{at}\mathrm{d}t\,.

It is easy to check that for
2\leq j\leq p

\iota_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}=\frac{\sqrt{2}(e^{-a/p}-1)}{|a|}\,\left(1-e^{an}\right)\,\left(\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\right)-1\right)\,,

(7.13)

where
\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z)={\cal R}e(z)
for even
j
and

\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z)={\cal I}m(z)
for odd
j
.
For even
j
, in view of (
7.8
), one gets the inequality

|\iota_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|\,\leq\,\iota_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,\left(p^{-1}+j^{-2}\right)\,,\quad\iota_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=\sqrt{2}\,a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\,e^{2a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}+3\,e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,.

(7.14)

For odd
3\leq j\leq p
one has the estimate

\left|{\cal I}m\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{{\bf q}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\right|\leq\frac{p}{j}\,,

which implies that

|\iota_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}|\,\leq\,\sqrt{2}\,e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\,\left(p^{-1}+j^{-1}\right)\leq\,\iota_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,\left(p^{-1}+j^{-1}\right)\,.

(7.15)

Therefore,

\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,|\tau_{\mathchoice{2,j}{2,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,j$}}}|\leq a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\,\iota^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,\left(p^{-2}+j^{-2}\right)\,,\quad 2\leq j\leq p\,.

From here and the definition of
\tau_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
in (
7.2
),
we obtain

\sup_{\mathchoice{n\geq 1}{n\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\geq 1$}}}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,|\tau_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\leq\tau_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\left(1+\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq 1}{j\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq 1$}}}\,j^{-2}\right)\leq 3\tau_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\,.

So, in view of (
7.2
),
for any any
n\geq 1
and
Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

the term
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)\leq 3\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\tau_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
and
\tau_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\left(1+e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}+2\,(\sqrt{2}a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}e^{a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}+3)^{2}\,e^{2a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}}\right)
.
Taking into account that
\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\leq\varsigma^{*}
, we get
the upper bound
(
7.1
). Hence Proposition
7.1
.

∎

Proposition 7.2
.

There exists a constant
{\bf l}>0
such that
for any
n\geq 1

\sup_{\mathchoice{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(Q)\leq{\bf l}(1+(\varsigma^{*})^{2})\,,

(7.16)

where the parameter
\varsigma^{*}
is defined (
2.7
).

Proof.
To estimate the total weighted noise deviation (
4.9
)
note that for
x\in{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\left(\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\widetilde{\xi}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{2}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\overline{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(x)\,,

(7.17)

where

\overline{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(x)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})=I^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})-{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\,.

Using
Proposition
A.8
, the definition of
\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}
in (
2.7
),
and that
\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}=2
, we obtain that there exists a constant
{\bf l}>0
such that for any
3\leq p\leq n

{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(Q)\leq{\bf l}\left(1+\sigma^{2}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}+\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\right)

(7.18)

where

\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2}{i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\psi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}},\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})

and

\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(f,g)=\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq v+t\leq n}{0\leq v+t\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}}\,\left(\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}f(u+v)g(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|+\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}g(u+v)f(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|\right)\,.

Denoting

\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq v+t\leq n}{0\leq v+t\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}}\,\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(u+v)\psi_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|\,,

we obtain that

\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq 2\sup_{\mathchoice{l\geq 3,j\geq 3,|l-j|\geq 2}{l\geq 3,j\geq 3,|l-j|\geq 2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l\geq 3,j\geq 3,|l-j|\geq 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l\geq 3,j\geq 3,|l-j|\geq 2$}}}\,\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}
.
To estimate the term
\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}

note that for any
3\leq j,l\leq p

\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq v\leq 1}{0\leq v\leq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq v\leq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq v\leq 1$}}}\,\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq t\leq n-v}{0\leq t\leq n-v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n-v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n-v$}}}\,\left|[t]{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)+\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{[t]}{[t]}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle[t]$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle[t]$}}}\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(u+v)\,\psi_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(u)\,\mathrm{d}u\right|\,

with
{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)=\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(u+v)\,\psi_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(u)\,\mathrm{d}u
.
Therefore,

\upsilon_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\leq 2+n\,\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq v\leq 1}{0\leq v\leq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq v\leq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq v\leq 1$}}}\,\left|{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)\right|\,.

(7.19)

Taking into account the definition of the functions
\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
in (
3.5
) for the trigonometric basis (
3.3
), we find that
for
t_{\mathchoice{s-1}{s-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s-1$}}}\leq v\leq t_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}
the function
{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)

can be represented as

{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)=(t_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}-v)\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(t_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}})\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}+t_{\mathchoice{s-1}{s-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s-1$}}})+(v-t_{\mathchoice{s-1}{s-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s-1$}}})\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(t_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}})\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t_{\mathchoice{k}{k}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k$}}}+t_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}})\,.

We recall that for any
u,v\geq 0
the functions
(
3.3
) satisfy the following equation

\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(u+v)={\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,1}{j,1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,1$}}}(v)\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j-1}{j-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j-1$}}}(u)+{\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,2}{j,2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,2$}}}(v)\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(u)+{\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,3}{j,3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,3$}}}(v)\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j+1}{j+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j+1$}}}(u)\,,

where
{\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,1}{j,1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,1$}}}(\cdot)
,
{\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,2}{j,2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,2$}}}(\cdot)
and
{\bf k}^{*}_{\mathchoice{j,3}{j,3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,3$}}}(\cdot)
are some trigonometric functions. Using the property
(
3.3
) we obtain that for any
v>0
for
|l-j|\geq 2
the function
{\bf V}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}(v)=0
, i.e.
\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq 4
. Using this in
(
7.18
) and taking into account that
\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\leq\varsigma^{*}
, we obtain the inequality (
7.16
).
Hence Proposition
7.2
.

∎

8
Proofs

8.1
Proof of Proposition
3.1

Proof.
First, we represent the process (
2.5
)
as

\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\varrho_{1}\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(1)}+\varrho_{2}\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(2)}\,,

where
(\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(1)})_{\mathchoice{t\geq 0}{t\geq 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq 0$}}}
and
(\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(2)})_{\mathchoice{t\geq 0}{t\geq 0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq 0$}}}
are independent
Ornstein–Uhlenbeck processes

\mathrm{d}\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(1)}=a\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(1)}dt+\mathrm{d}w_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\mathrm{d}\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(2)}=a\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(2)}dt+\mathrm{d}z_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}

with
\xi^{(1)}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}=\xi^{(2)}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}=0
.
Moreover, for any square integrable function
f
we denote

I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(1)}(f)=\int_{0}^{t}f(s)d\xi_{s}^{(1)}\quad\mbox{and}\quad I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}^{(2)}(f)=\int_{0}^{t}f(s)d\xi_{s}^{(2)}\,.

(8.1)

Then the covariance matrix (
3.8
) can be rewritten in the form

{\bf G}=\varrho_{1}^{2}\,{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+\varrho_{2}^{2}{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}},

and the
(i,j)
-th elements of the matrix
{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}
and
{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}
are defined as

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}I_{n}^{(1)}\,(\psi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})I_{n}^{(1)}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})
and
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}I_{n}^{(2)}\,(\psi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})I_{n}^{(2)}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})

with
\psi_{j}(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{np}\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t_{k}){\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{(t_{k-1},t_{k}]}{(t_{k-1},t_{k}]}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(t_{k-1},t_{k}]$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(t_{k-1},t_{k}]$}}}(t)
.
Applying the celebrated inequality
of Lidskii and Wieland (see, for example, in

[
30
]
, G.3.a., p.334)
yields

\mbox{tr}{\bf G}-\lambda_{\max}({\bf G})\geq\varrho_{1}^{2}(\mbox{tr}\,{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}-\lambda_{\mathchoice{\max}{\max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\max$}}}({\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}))\quad\mbox{a.s.}

(8.2)

Now, using
Proposition
A.1

with
\varrho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=1
and

\varrho_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=0
,
we obtain that

\mbox{tr}{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}(I_{n}^{(1)}(\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}))^{2}=d+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,b_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,

(8.3)

where

b_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\frac{a}{n}\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}\,\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{t}e^{a(t-s)}\,\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(s)(1+e^{2as})\mathrm{d}s\mathrm{d}t\,.

Setting
\Phi(t,v)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t-v)
,
we get

\displaystyle\mbox{tr}{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}

\displaystyle=d+\frac{a}{n}\int_{0}^{n}e^{av}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{v}{v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle v$}}}^{n}\Phi(t,v)\left(1+e^{2a(t-v)}\right)\mathrm{d}t\right)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle>d-2|a|\int_{0}^{n}e^{av}\,\Phi^{*}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(v)\mathrm{d}v\quad\mbox{and}\quad\Phi^{*}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(v)=\max_{\mathchoice{t\geq v}{t\geq v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq v$}}}|\Phi(t,v)|\,.

Note, that the function
\Phi^{*}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(\cdot)
is
1
- periodic, therefore, in view of the bound (
A.29
)

\mbox{tr}{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}>d-2|a|\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{k=1}{k=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle k=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle k=1$}}}\,e^{a(k-1)}\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{1}\,\Phi^{*}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(v)\mathrm{d}v>d-\frac{5+\ln d}{2\check{a}}\,,

where
\check{a}
is defined in (
3.10
). Therefore,

\mbox{tr}{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}>d/2
for
d\geq d_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
.
Now, note that

\lambda_{\mathchoice{\max}{\max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\max$}}}({\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})=\sup_{\mathchoice{|z|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=1}{|z|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|z|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|z|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=1$}}}n^{-1}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,(I_{n}^{(1)}({\bf s}))^{2}\,,

where
{\bf s}(t)=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,z_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\psi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)
.
Using
Proposition
A.1
, we find that for
|z|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=1

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,(I_{n}^{(1)}({\bf s}))^{2}=2a\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}{\bf s}(t)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}({\bf s})\mathrm{d}t+\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}\,{\bf s}^{2}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t=2a\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}{\bf s}(t)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}({\bf s})\mathrm{d}t+n\,.

For
a\leq 0
through the Cauchy - Bunyakovsky - Schwarz inequality
we obtain

\displaystyle 2\left|a\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}{\bf s}(t)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}({\bf s})\mathrm{d}t\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2|a|\,\int_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}^{n}\,e^{au}\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}({\bf s})\left(\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{u}{u}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle u$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle u$}}}\,|{\bf s}(t)||{\bf s}(t-u)|\mathrm{d}t\right)\mathrm{d}u|

\displaystyle\leq 2\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf s}^{2}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t=2n\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf s}^{2}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t=2n\,,

i.e.
\lambda_{\mathchoice{\max}{\max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\max$}}}({\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\leq 3
. Hence Proposition
3.1
.

∎

8.2
Proof of Theorem
5.1

Substituting (
4.13
) in (
4.10
) yields for any
\gamma\in\Gamma

\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma)=J(\gamma)+2\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)\left(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\frac{\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n}-\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)+\|S\|^{2}-\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)\,.

(8.4)

Now we put
L(\gamma)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)
,

M(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,\quad M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)g(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,

(8.5)

B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}^{2}-\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}})\,\quad\mbox{and}\quad B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\gamma)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)(\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}^{2})\,.

(8.6)

Taking into account the definition (
4.14
), we can rewrite (
8.4
) as

\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma)\,=\,J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+2\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n}L(\gamma)+2\,M(\gamma)+\frac{2}{n}B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)

+2\sqrt{P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)}\frac{B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\overline{\gamma})}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}n}}-2M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)\,+\,\|S\|^{2}-\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)

(8.7)

with
\overline{\gamma}=\gamma/|\gamma|_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
. Let
\gamma_{0}=(\gamma_{0}(j))_{1\leq j\leq p}
be a fixed sequence in
\Gamma
and
\gamma^{*}
be as in (
4.16
).
Substituting
\gamma_{0}
and
\gamma^{*}
in (
8.7
), we consider the difference

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})-\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}-\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n}L(x)+2M(x)+\frac{2}{n}B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(x)

\displaystyle+2\sqrt{P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})}\frac{B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\overline{\gamma^{*}})}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}n}}-2\sqrt{P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})}\frac{B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\overline{\gamma_{0}})}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}n}}

\displaystyle-2M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma^{*})+2M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma_{0})-\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\,,

where
x=\gamma^{*}-\gamma_{0}
.
One has that
|L(x)|\leq 2\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
and
|B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(x)|\leq{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)
.
Applying the elementary inequality

2|ab|\leq\varepsilon a^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}b^{2}

(8.8)

with any
\varepsilon>0
, we get

2\sqrt{P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)}\frac{B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\overline{\gamma})}{\sqrt{\sigma_{Q}n}}\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+\frac{B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}^{2}(\overline{\gamma})}{\varepsilon\sigma_{Q}n}\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+\frac{B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{\varepsilon\sigma n}\,,

where

B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=\max_{\mathchoice{\gamma\in\Gamma}{\gamma\in\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\in\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\in\Gamma$}}}\,\left(B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}^{2}(\overline{\gamma})+B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}^{2}(\overline{\gamma}^{2})\right)

with
\gamma^{2}=(\gamma_{j}^{2})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq n}{1\leq j\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}}
.
From the definition of the function
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(Q)
in condition
{\bf C}_{2})

we obtain

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\leq\sum_{\mathchoice{\gamma\in\Gamma}{\gamma\in\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma\in\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma\in\Gamma$}}}\,\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}^{2}(\overline{\gamma})+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}^{2}(\overline{\gamma}^{2})\right)\leq 2\nu{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(Q)\,.

(8.9)

Moreover, by the same method we estimate the term
M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}
.
Note that

\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,g^{2}_{\mathchoice{\gamma}{\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma$}}}(j)\,\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}={\bf c}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq\frac{{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n}\,,

(8.10)

where
{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=n\max_{\gamma\in\Gamma}{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}^{2}
.
Therefore,
through the Cauchy-Bunyakovsky-Schwarz inequality we can estimate the term
M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)
as

|M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)|\leq\frac{|\gamma|_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\sqrt{n}}{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\left(\sum_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}^{n}\overline{\gamma}^{2}(j)\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{1/2}=\frac{|\gamma|_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\sqrt{n}}{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\left(\sigma_{Q}+B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\overline{\gamma}^{2})\right)^{1/2}\,.

So, applying the elementary inequality (
8.8
) with some arbitrary
\varepsilon>0
, we have

2|M_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma)|\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+\frac{{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\varepsilon\sigma_{Q}n}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})\,.

Using the bounds above, one finds

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})+\frac{4\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}+2M(x)+\frac{2}{n}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\varepsilon}\,\frac{{\bf c}^{*}}{n\sigma_{Q}}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})+\frac{2}{\varepsilon}\,\frac{B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}}{n\sigma_{Q}}

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+2\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})-\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\,.

The setting
\varepsilon=\rho/4

and the estimating where this is possible
\rho
by
1

in this inequality
imply

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})+\frac{5\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}+2M(x)+\frac{2}{n}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)

\displaystyle+\frac{16({\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+1)(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{\rho n\sigma_{Q}}-\frac{\rho}{2}\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\frac{\rho}{2}P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})+\rho\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\,.

Moreover, taking into account here that

|\widehat{P}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})-P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})|\leq\frac{\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}

and
\rho<1/2
,
we obtain that

\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})\leq\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})+\frac{6\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}+2M(x)+\frac{2}{n}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)\\[5.69054pt]
+\frac{16({\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+1)(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{\rho n\sigma_{Q}}-\frac{\rho}{2}P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\frac{3\rho}{2}P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\,.

(8.11)

Now we examine the third term in the right-hand side of this inequality. We have

2|M(x)|\leq\varepsilon\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}+\frac{Z^{*}}{n\varepsilon}\,,

(8.12)

where
S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}

and

Z^{*}=\sup_{x\in\Gamma_{1}}\frac{nM^{2}(x)}{\|S_{x}\|^{2}}\,.

We remind that the set
\Gamma_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=\Gamma-\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}
.
Using the property
(
2.2
),
we get for any fixed
x=(x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq n}{1\leq j\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,M^{2}(x)=\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\left(S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\right)}{n^{2}}\leq\frac{\varkappa_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}}{n}=\frac{\varkappa_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}}{n}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\bar{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,p}{j,p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,p$}}}

(8.13)

and, therefore,

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}Z^{*}\leq\sum_{x\in\Gamma_{1}}\frac{nM^{2}(x)}{\|S_{x}\|^{2}}\leq\varkappa_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\nu\,.

(8.14)

Besides, the norm
\|S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{*}}{\gamma^{*}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}}-S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}\|
can be estimated from below as

\|S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma}{\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma$}}}-S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}\|^{2}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}(x(j)+\beta(j))^{2}\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\geq\|\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}+2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}x(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,

where
\beta(j)=\gamma_{0}(j)g_{j}(\gamma_{0})-\gamma(j)g_{j}(\gamma)
.
Then, in view of (
3.7
)

\displaystyle\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\|S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma}{\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma$}}}-S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}\|^{2}\leq\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}-\|\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}-2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-2M(x^{2})-2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\Upsilon(x)\,,

where
\Upsilon(\gamma)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
. The first term in this inequality we can estimate as

2M(x^{2})\leq\varepsilon\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}+\frac{Z_{1}^{*}}{n\varepsilon}\quad\mbox{and}\quad Z^{*}_{1}=\sup_{x\in\Gamma_{1}}\frac{nM^{2}(x^{2})}{\|S_{x}\|^{2}}\,.

Similarly to (
8.14
), we can estimate the last term as

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}Z_{1}^{*}\leq\sigma_{Q}\nu\,.

From this it follows that for any
0<\varepsilon<1

\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon}\left(\|S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma}{\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma$}}}-S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}\|^{2}+\frac{Z_{1}^{*}}{n\varepsilon}-2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\frac{2\Upsilon(x)}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\,.

(8.15)

The property
(
8.10
) yields

\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\beta^{2}(j)\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq 2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,g^{2}_{\mathchoice{\gamma}{\gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma$}}}(j)\,\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,g^{2}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}$}}}(j)\,\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq\frac{4{\bf c}^{*}}{\varepsilon n}\,.

(8.16)

Taking into account that
|x(j)|\leq 1
and using the inequality (
8.8
), we get
for any
\varepsilon>0

2\left|\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x(j)\beta(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\bar{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right|\leq\varepsilon\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}+\frac{4{\bf c}^{*}}{\varepsilon n}\,.

To estimate the last term in the right hand of (
8.15
) we use first
the Cauchy-Bunyakovsky-Schwarz inequality
and then the bound
(
8.16
), i.e.

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}|\Upsilon(\gamma)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\beta^{2}(j)\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\bar{\gamma}^{2}(j)\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+\frac{{\bf c}^{*}}{n\varepsilon\sigma_{Q}}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\bar{\gamma}^{2}(j)\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)+\frac{{\bf c}^{*}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{n\varepsilon\sigma_{Q}}\,.

Therefore,

\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}|\Upsilon(x)|\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}|\Upsilon(\gamma^{*})|+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}|\Upsilon(\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})|\leq\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}})+\frac{2{\bf c}^{*}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{n\varepsilon\sigma_{Q}}\,.

So, using all these bounds in (
8.15
),
we obtain

\|S_{\mathchoice{x}{x}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x$}}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\varepsilon)}\biggl(\frac{Z_{1}^{*}}{n\varepsilon}+\|S_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{*}}{\gamma^{*}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}}^{*}-S_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{0}}{\gamma_{0}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{6{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\sigma+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{n\sigma\varepsilon}+\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+\varepsilon P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\biggr)\,.

Using in the inequality (
8.12
) this bound and the estimate

\|S_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{*}}{\gamma^{*}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{*}$}}}^{*}-S_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{0}}{\gamma_{0}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}}^{*}\|^{2}\leq 2(\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})+\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0}))\,,

we have

\displaystyle 2|M(x)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Z^{*}+Z_{1}^{*}}{n(1-\varepsilon)\varepsilon}+\frac{2\varepsilon(\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})+\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0}))}{(1-\varepsilon)}

\displaystyle+\frac{6{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{n\sigma_{Q}(1-\varepsilon)}+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{1-\varepsilon}\left(P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\right)\,.

Choosing here
\varepsilon\leq\rho/2<1/2
, we find

\displaystyle 2|M(x)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2(Z^{*}+Z_{1}^{*})}{n\varepsilon}+\frac{2\varepsilon(\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})+\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0}))}{(1-\varepsilon)}

\displaystyle+\frac{12{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\sigma_{Q}+B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})}{n\sigma_{Q}}+\varepsilon\left(P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma^{*})+P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})\right)\,.

From here and (
8.11
), it follows that

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1-3\varepsilon}\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})+\frac{6\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n(1-3\varepsilon)}+\frac{2}{n(1-3\varepsilon)}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)
