\displaystyle+\frac{28(1+{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})(B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+\sigma_{Q})}{\rho(1-3\varepsilon)n\sigma_{Q}}+\frac{2(Z^{*}+Z_{1}^{*})}{n(1-3\varepsilon)}+\frac{2\rho P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})}{1-3\varepsilon}.

Putting here
\varepsilon=\rho/3

and estimating
(1-\rho)^{-1}
by
2
where this is possible,
we get

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\rho/3}{1-\rho}\mbox{Err}\,(\gamma_{0})+\frac{12\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}+\frac{4}{n}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)

\displaystyle+\frac{56(1+{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})(B^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+\sigma_{Q})}{\rho n\sigma_{Q}}+\frac{4(Z^{*}+Z_{1}^{*})}{n}+\frac{2\rho P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})}{1-\rho}\,.

Taking the expectation and using the upper bound for
P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma_{0})
in Lemma
A.10

with
\varepsilon=\rho
yields

{\cal R}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}(S^{*},S)\leq\frac{1+5\rho}{1-\rho}{\cal R}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}(S^{*}_{\mathchoice{\gamma_{0}}{\gamma_{0}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma_{0}$}}},S)+\frac{{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n\rho}+\frac{12\nu_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}|\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-\sigma_{Q}|}{n}\,,

where

{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=4{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}+56(1+c^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})(2{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\nu+1)+2{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
and the terms
{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(Q)
and
{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q\in{\cal Q}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(Q)
.
The inequality holds for each
\gamma_{0}\in\Gamma
, this implies Theorem
5.1
.

∎

8.3
Proof of Proposition
5.2

Proof.
Using the regression scheme (
3.7
) in (
4.12
),
we obtain that

\widehat{\sigma}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\frac{n}{p}\sum_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}^{n}\widehat{{\bf t}}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\frac{n}{p}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}\bar{{\bf t}}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+\frac{2n}{p}M(\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{1}{p}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}

(8.17)

where the term
M(\gamma)
is defined in (
8.5
),

\gamma^{\prime}=(\gamma^{\prime}(j))_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq p}{1\leq j\leq p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq p$}}}
and
\gamma^{\prime}(j)={\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{j\geq[\sqrt{n}]+1\}}{\{j\geq[\sqrt{n}]+1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{j\geq[\sqrt{n}]+1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{j\geq[\sqrt{n}]+1\}$}}}
. Note here, that the noise coefficients

\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
are defined for the trigonometric basis. The first term we estimate trough Lemma A.3 from
[
26
]
, i.e.
we have

\frac{n}{p}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}\bar{{\bf t}}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq\frac{8n}{p}\left(\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}|\dot{S}(t)|\mathrm{d}t\right)^{2}\sum_{\mathchoice{j>[\sqrt{n}]}{j>[\sqrt{n}]}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j>[\sqrt{n}]$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j>[\sqrt{n}]$}}}\,j^{-2}\leq\frac{8n\|\dot{S}\|^{2}}{[\sqrt{n}]p}\,.

Moreover, note now that

M(\gamma^{\prime})=\frac{1}{n}\,I_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{\prime}}{\gamma^{\prime}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}})\quad\mbox{and}\quad S_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{\prime}}{\gamma^{\prime}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{\prime}(j)\bar{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,.

Therefore, the property (
2.2
) and condition

{\bf H}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})

provide that for some constant
{\bf l}>0

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,M^{2}(\gamma^{\prime})\leq\frac{\varkappa^{*}}{n^{2}}\,\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,S^{2}_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{\prime}}{\gamma^{\prime}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}}(t)\mathrm{d}t=\frac{\varkappa^{*}}{n}\|S_{\mathchoice{\gamma^{\prime}}{\gamma^{\prime}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\gamma^{\prime}$}}}\|^{2}=\frac{\varkappa^{*}}{n}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}\bar{{\bf t}}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq{\bf l}\frac{\|\dot{S}\|^{2}}{n\sqrt{n}}\,.

As to the last term in the representation
(
8.17
), we note that

\frac{1}{p}\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{j=[\sqrt{n}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=[\sqrt{n}]+1$}}}\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\frac{p-\sqrt{n}}{p}\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}+\frac{1}{p}B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\gamma^{\prime\prime})\,,

where

\gamma^{\prime\prime}=\gamma^{\prime}/\sqrt{p}
and the functions
B_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\cdot)
,
B_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}(\cdot)
are defined
in (
8.6
).
The using conditions
{\bf H}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})
,
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}
) –
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}
)
and
{\bf D}
) implies the upper bound (
5.3
). Hence Proposition
5.2
.

∎

A
Appendix

A.1
Stochastic calculus for the non-Gaussian Ornstein–Uhlenbeck–Lévy processes

In this section we study the process (
2.5
).

Proposition A.1
.

Let
f
and
g
be two nonrandom left continuous
{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathchoice{+}{+}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle+$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}\to{\mathbb{R}}
functions
with the finite right limits.
Then for any
t>0

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)\,,

(A.1)

where the variance
\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}
is defined in(
2.7
),

\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\left(f(s)g(s)+\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)g(s)+f(s)\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)\right)\,\mathrm{d}s

and

\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,f(s)\left(\frac{1+e^{2as}}{2}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,.

Proof.
From the definitions
(
3.7
) and (
2.5
)
we obtain through the Ito formula that

\displaystyle I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)g(s)\mathrm{d}s+a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\mathrm{d}s+{\bf M}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)\,,

(A.2)

where
\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)=f(s)I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)+g(s)I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)
,

{\bf M}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{s-}{s-}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s-$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s-$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}u_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varrho^{2}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}f(s)\,g(s)\,\mathrm{d}m_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}

and
m_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=x^{2}*(\mu-\widetilde{\mu})_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}
.
Moreover, using the Ito formula, we get

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\frac{e^{2at}-1}{2a}\,.

(A.3)

Note now that

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)

\displaystyle={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\left(a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\xi_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s+\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\,\mathrm{d}u_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2a^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\mathrm{d}s\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf E}\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\mathrm{d}s+2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\mathrm{d}s\,.

So, from here

\sup_{\mathchoice{0\leq t\leq n}{0\leq t\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\leq 2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,(|a|+1)\,\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\mathrm{d}s<\infty\,.

(A.4)

This implies immediately that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,{\bf M}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)=0
. Using this in (
A.2
) yields

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)g(s)\mathrm{d}s+a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\left(f(s)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\zeta_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)+g(s)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\zeta_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,,

(A.5)

where
\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\xi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\,I_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)
. Therefore, putting
g=1
in (
A.5
), we have

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\mathrm{d}s+a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\left(f(s){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(1)+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,.

Taking into account here that
\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}
, we find

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}\,e^{a(t-s)}f(s)\frac{1+e^{2as}}{2}\mathrm{d}s=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,.

Therefore, using this in
(
A.5
), we obtain (
A.1
).

∎

Corollary A.2
.

For any càdlàg function
f
from
{\bf L}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}[0,n]

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(f)\leq 2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{n}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\,\mathrm{d}s\,.

(A.6)

This corollary follows directly from Proposition
A.1
through the Cauchy - Bunyakovsky - Schwarz inequality.

Now we set

\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=I^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)-{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\quad\mbox{and}\quad V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,-{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,.

(A.7)

Using (
A.2
) with
f=g
, one has

\mathrm{d}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=2af(t)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}t+\mathrm{d}\widetilde{M}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,,

(A.8)

where

\widetilde{M}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=M_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,f)
.
To study this process we set

\check{\tau}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)=f(t)g(t)\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,1)+f(t)\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,g)+g(t)\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,f)+\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)

(A.9)

and

A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}e^{3a(t-s)}f(s)\upsilon(s)\mathrm{d}s+2\,\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}e^{3a(t-s)}\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}s\,,

(A.10)

where
\upsilon(s)=a^{2}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{\xi}^{2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\left(e^{2as}-1\right)+a\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}
,

\widetilde{\xi}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}=\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}-{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}
and

\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=\varrho^{4}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,\Pi(x^{4})
.

Proposition A.3
.

For any
left continuous functions with finite right limits
f
and
g

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{2a(t-s)}\,H_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}s

(A.11)

where

H_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)=g(t)A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)+f(t)A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)+\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\check{\tau}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,f(t)g(t)
.

Proof.
Applying again (
A.2
) with
g=1

yields

\mathrm{d}V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=aV_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}t+a\,f(t)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\mathrm{d}t+\mathrm{d}\,L_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,,

(A.12)

where

L_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s-}{s-}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s-$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s-$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}u_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\varrho^{2}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\mathrm{d}m_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}
and

\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)=f(s)\xi_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)
.
By the Ito formula we get

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)

\displaystyle=2aV_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\mathrm{d}t+a\left(g(t)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)+f(t)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\right)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle+\mathrm{d}\,[L(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}+V_{\mathchoice{t-}{t-}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t-$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t-$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}L_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)+V_{\mathchoice{t-}{t-}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t-$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t-$}}}(g)\mathrm{d}L_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,.

Now, in view of Lemma A.2 from
[
35
]
, we find

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)

\displaystyle=2a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\mathrm{d}t+\left(g(t)A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)+f(t)A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\right)\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle+\mathrm{d}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[L(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,,

(A.13)

where
A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)
.
Note that
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\check{\tau}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)

and

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[L(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}

\displaystyle=\varrho^{2}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,\check{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)\,\mathrm{d}s+{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\sum_{\mathchoice{0\leq s\leq t}{0\leq s\leq t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq s\leq t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq s\leq t$}}}\,\Delta\,L_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\Delta\,L_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\check{\tau}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}s+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)g(s)\mathrm{d}s\,.

To find
A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)
we put
g=1
in (
A.9
).
Note that
A_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=\widetilde{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\widetilde{\xi}^{2}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}
. Then

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{3a(t-s)}\,\left(a\,f(s)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{\xi}^{2}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}+\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\check{\tau}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,1)+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,f(s)\right)\mathrm{d}s\,.

Using here that

a\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,1)=(e^{2at}-1)/2\quad\mbox{and}\quad a\,\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,f)=\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,,

(A.14)

we obtain the representation (
A.11
). Hence Proposition
A.3
.

∎

Proposition A.4
.

For any
left continuous function
f
with finite right limits

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{2a(t-s)}\,\widetilde{\varkappa}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,\mathrm{d}s\,,

(A.15)

where

\widetilde{\varkappa}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)=2f(s)\,A_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,+4\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,f(s)\tau_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,1)+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,f^{2}(s)
.

Proof.
Using the Ito formula and Lemma A.2 from
[
35
]
,
we get for any bounded nonrandom functions
f
and
g

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)

\displaystyle=a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\,\mathrm{d}t+2af(t){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle+a\,g(t)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\mathrm{d}t+\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,.

(A.16)

Putting here
g=1
and taking into account that
V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)
,
we have

\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)=2a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\,\mathrm{d}t+2af(t){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1)\,\mathrm{d}t+\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(1)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,.

Taking into account here that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(1)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\check{A}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}s
, we come to the equality (
A.15
).

∎

Now, for integrable
[0,+\infty)\to{\mathbb{R}}
functions
f
and
g
we define the correlation measure as

\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(f,g)=\max_{\mathchoice{0\leq v+t\leq n}{0\leq v+t\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq v+t\leq n$}}}\,\left(\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}f(u+v)g(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|+\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}g(u+v)f(u)\mathrm{d}u\right|\right)

(A.17)

Proposition A.5
.

Let
f
and
g
be two
left continuous bounded by
\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
functions with finite right limits, i.e.
\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}

and
\|g\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
, where
\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{0\leq t\leq n}{0\leq t\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0\leq t\leq n$}}}|f(t)|
.
Then for any
0\leq t\leq n

\left|a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\right|\leq u^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,g)+u^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)+u^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}\,,

(A.18)

where
u^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=4\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}(a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}})\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+3\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}
,

u^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=44\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}
and
u^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}=3\phi^{3}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}
.

Proof.
First, note that from Ito formula
we find

a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)=a^{2}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,g(s)\,\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(1)\right)\mathrm{d}s\\[5.69054pt]
+2a^{2}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,f(s)\,\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)\,V_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\right)\mathrm{d}s+a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\,.

(A.19)

Using here
Lemmas A.4
and A.6 from
[
35
]
,
we obtain

|a{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\,V_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)|\leq 15\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\|g\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\,.

(A.20)

One can check directly that

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}

\displaystyle=2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,g(s)f(s)\,\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(1)\right)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\,\left({\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,I_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)\right)\mathrm{d}s+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\,g(s)\mathrm{d}s\,.

From (
A.1
) we find that

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}

\displaystyle=2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,g(s)f(s)\,\tau_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,1)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)\,\tau_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}s+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)\,g(s)\mathrm{d}s\,.

Using the last equality in (
A.14
),
we have

\displaystyle a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}=2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,g(s)f(s)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+2\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,f(s)\,\tau_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}s+a\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,f^{2}(s)\,g(s)\mathrm{d}s\,.

Note now that

\check{\varepsilon}^{\prime}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)=a\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)+af(t)(1+e^{2at})/2\,,

i.e.

\|\check{\varepsilon}^{\prime}(f)\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\leq 2|a|\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}
. Therefore, in view of Lemma A.3 from
[
35
]

we get

\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,g(s)f(s)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}s\right|\leq 4\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\,.

By integrating by parts we can obtain

\left|\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,g(s)\,\check{\varepsilon}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)\mathrm{d}s\right|\leq\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)
,
and, therefore,

|\tau_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)|\leq 3\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)\,.

(A.21)

So, the last term in (
A.19
)
can be estimated as

\left|\,a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,e^{a(t-s)}\,\mathrm{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,L(g)]_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}\right|\leq 14\,\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)\,\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\|g\|_{\mathchoice{*,t}{*,t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,t$}}}\,.

Using Lemma A.5 from
[
35
]
in (
A.19
)
we come to the bound (
A.18
).

∎

Proposition A.6
.

Let
f
and
g
be two
left continuous bounded by
\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
functions with finite right limits, i.e.
\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}

and
\|g\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
. Then for any
t>0

\left|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,\widetilde{M}(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}\,\right|\leq\left(12\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)+\phi^{4}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\right)t\,.

(A.22)

Proof.
From (
A.1
)
we obtain that

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,[\widetilde{M}(f)\,,\,\widetilde{M}(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}=4\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f(s)g(s)\tau_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f,g)\,\mathrm{d}s+\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\,\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,f^{2}(s)g^{2}(s)\,\mathrm{d}s\,.

(A.23)

Using here the bound (
A.21
)
we get (
A.22
). Hence Proposition
A.6
.

∎

Corollary A.7
.

Let
f
and
g
be two
left continuous bounded by
\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
functions with finite right limits, i.e.
\|f\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}

and
\|g\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
. Then for any
t>0

\left|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)\right|\,\leq\,\left(v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,f)+\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(1,g))+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f,g)+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}\right)\,t\,,

(A.24)

where
v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=8\phi^{3}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}a_{\mathchoice{max}{max}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle max$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle max$}}}\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+6\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}
,

v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}=100\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sigma_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}^{2}
and
v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}=13\phi^{4}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\check{\varrho}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}
.

Proof.
From (
A.8
) by the Ito formula one finds for
t\geq 0

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(f)\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}(g)={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}[\widetilde{M}(f),\widetilde{M}(g)]_{\mathchoice{t}{t}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}}}+2a\int^{t}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\left(f(s){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)V_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)+g(s){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(f)V_{\mathchoice{s}{s}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}}}(g)\right)\mathrm{d}s\,.

Using here Proposition
A.5
and Proposition
A.6
we come to desire result.

∎

Now for square integrable functions
(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq n}{1\leq j\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq n$}}}

and
x=(x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}},\ldots,x_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}})\in[0,1]^{p}

we set

\overline{I}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}(x)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\,.

(A.25)

We need to study this total deviation.

Proposition A.8
.

Assume that
\phi_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\equiv 1
and the functions
(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})_{\mathchoice{2\leq j\leq n}{2\leq j\leq n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2\leq j\leq n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2\leq j\leq n$}}}
are bounded, i.e.

\max_{\mathchoice{2\leq j\leq p}{2\leq j\leq p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2\leq j\leq p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2\leq j\leq p$}}}\|\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\|_{\mathchoice{*,n}{*,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*,n$}}}\leq\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
.
Then, for any
p\geq 3
and
n\geq 1

\sup_{\mathchoice{x\in{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}}{x\in{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x\in{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x\in{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\overline{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}(x)\leq 4n^{2}\,(4\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+5\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+2v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}})+2np\left(\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(2v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}\right),

(A.26)

where
\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2}{i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i\geq 3,j\geq 3,|i-j|\geq 2$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}},\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})

and
the ball
{\cal B}_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}
is defined in
(
4.9
).

Proof.
We
represent the sum (
A.25
) as

\overline{I}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}(x)=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})=x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})+x_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})+J_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}\,,

where
J_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}=\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{j=3}{j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=3$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})
. In view of
x^{2}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+x^{2}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\leq 1
, one has

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\overline{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(x)\,\leq 2{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})+2{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,\widetilde{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})+2{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,J^{2}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}\,.

Using
Corollary
A.7

and taking into account that

\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}},\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\leq 2\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}n
if
|i-j|\leq 1
, we get

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\overline{I}^{2}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(x)\,\leq\,4n^{2}\left(4\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+2\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}\right)+2{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,J^{2}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}

(A.27)

and

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\,J^{2}_{\mathchoice{p,n}{p,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p,n$}}}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{i,j=3}{i,j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j=3$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}}x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q}{Q}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q$}}}\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})\widetilde{I}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\leq n\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{i,j=3}{i,j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j=3$}}}|x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}}||x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\widetilde{\kappa}_{\mathchoice{i,j}{i,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j$}}}\,,

(A.28)

where
