\widetilde{\kappa}_{\mathchoice{i,j}{i,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j$}}}=v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(1,\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})+\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(1,\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}))+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}},\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}
.
Note here that
for any
i,j\geq 3
we can estimate the correlation coefficient as

\varpi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\phi_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}},\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})\leq 2\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}n{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|i-j|\leq 1\}}{\{|i-j|\leq 1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|i-j|\leq 1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|i-j|\leq 1\}$}}}+\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|i-j|\geq 2\}}{\{|i-j|\geq 2\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|i-j|\geq 2\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|i-j|\geq 2\}$}}}
. By making use of this estimate in
(
A.28
) and taking into account that

\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{i,j=3}{i,j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j=3$}}}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|i-j|\leq 1\}}{\{|i-j|\leq 1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|i-j|\leq 1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|i-j|\leq 1\}$}}}|x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}}||x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\leq 3\quad\mbox{and}\quad\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{i,j=3}{i,j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j=3$}}}|x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}}||x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\leq p\,,

one gets

\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{i,j=3}{i,j=3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j=3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j=3$}}}|x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}}||x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\widetilde{\kappa}_{\mathchoice{i,j}{i,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i,j$}}}\,\leq p\left(\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(2v^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})+v^{*}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}}\right)+6\phi_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}^{2}nv^{*}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}}.

From here and the bounds (
A.27
)–(
A.28
) we obtain (
A.26
).
Hence Proposition
A.8
.

∎

A.2
Property of the trigonometric basis

Lemma A.9
.

The
trigonometric basis (
3.3
) satisfies the following inequality

\sup_{\mathchoice{d\geq 1}{d\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d\geq 1$}}}\,\left(\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\max_{\mathchoice{t\geq v}{t\geq v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq v$}}}|\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)|-\ln d\right)\,\leq 5\,,

(A.29)

where
\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)\,\mbox{Tr}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t-v)
.

Proof.
Note that for any
d\geq 3
and
N=[d/2]

the sum
\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)
can be represented as

\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)=1+2\sum_{j=1}^{N}\cos(2\pi jv)-2\sin(2\pi Nt)\sin(2\pi N(t-v))\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{d=2N\}}{\{d=2N\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{d=2N\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{d=2N\}$}}}\,.

Therefore,

\max_{\mathchoice{t\geq v}{t\geq v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq v$}}}\,|\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)|\leq 2+|1+2\sum_{j=1}^{N}\cos(2\pi jv)|=2+\left|\frac{\sin(\pi(2N+1)v)}{\sin(\pi v)}\right|\,.

Using that
|1+2\sum_{j=1}^{N}\cos(2\pi jv)|\leq 2N+1\leq d
,
we obtain
for
0<\delta<1/2

\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\max_{\mathchoice{t\geq v}{t\geq v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq v$}}}\,|\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)|\mathrm{d}v\leq 2+2\delta(d+1)+2\int^{1/2}_{\mathchoice{\delta}{\delta}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\delta$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\delta$}}}\,\frac{1}{\sin(\pi v)}\mathrm{d}v\,.

In view of
\sin(\pi v)\geq 2v
for any
0<v<1/2
we get

\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\max_{\mathchoice{t\geq v}{t\geq v}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle t\geq v$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t\geq v$}}}\,|\Phi_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(t,v)|\,\mathrm{d}v\leq 4+2\delta d-\ln(2\delta)\,.

The minimizing this upper bound on
0<\delta<1
implies (
A.29
).
Hence Lemma
A.9
.

∎

A.3
Property of Penalty term

Lemma A.10
.

Assume that conditions
{\bf H}_{\mathchoice{2}{2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2$}}})
,
{\bf H}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}})
and
{\bf C}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})
hold. Then,
for any
n\geq 1
,
\gamma\in\Gamma
and
0<\varepsilon<1
the penalty term (
4.15
)
is bounded from above as

P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)\leq\frac{{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\mbox{Err}(\gamma)}{1-\varepsilon}+\frac{{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}}{n\varepsilon(1-\varepsilon)}+\frac{{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}}{n}\,,

(A.30)

where the terms
{\bf c}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
and
{\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}
are defined in (
4.5
) and (
4.8
) respectively.

Proof.
By the definition of
\mbox{Err}(\gamma)
one has

\displaystyle\mbox{Err}(\gamma)

\displaystyle=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,(\gamma(j)\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})^{2}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\left(\gamma(j)(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})+(\gamma(j)-1)\theta_{j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j)(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})^{2}+2\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)(\gamma(j)-1)\theta_{j}(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j}).

Taking into account the condition
{\bf H}_{\mathchoice{3}{3}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 3$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 3$}}})
and the definition
(
3.11
), we find

\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)(\gamma(j)-1)\theta_{j}(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)(\gamma(j)-1)\theta_{j}(\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})\,,

i.e.

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma(j)(\gamma(j)-1)\theta_{j}(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})=0
. So, in view of the inequality (
8.8
) we get

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\mbox{Err}(\gamma)

\displaystyle\geq\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,(\theta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\theta_{j})^{2}=\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\left(\frac{\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{\sqrt{n}}-g(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{n}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j)g(j)\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\xi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1-\varepsilon}{n}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j){\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\xi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,\gamma^{2}(j)g^{2}(j)\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,.

The using here here the upper bound (
4.8
) and the definition of
g(j)
in
(
3.11
) yields

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\mbox{Err}(\gamma)

\displaystyle\geq(1-\varepsilon)P_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(\gamma)-\frac{(1-\varepsilon){\bf L}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}}{n}-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{Q,S}{Q,S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle Q,S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle Q,S$}}}\,\sum^{p}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,g^{2}(j)\widehat{\theta}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,.

Now,
the inequality (
8.10
)
implies the bound (
A.30
). Hence Lemma
A.10
.

∎
